Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any clusters module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 428. The projective resolution has length at most 317 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the clusters decomposition. By minimality, the element a^428 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 317-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the clusters object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 17. The moduli space has dimension at most 349 with 2 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 17 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the clusters structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 349. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
